# Number Ledger — WP / EC / D allocation lock

The append-only reservation ledger that stops two concurrent sessions from
allocating the **same** WP / EC / D number (the failure that renumbered WP-419 →
WP-421). See **D-24245** (this mechanism; renumbered from D-24242 — see the D
section below) and the allocation protocol in
[`01.0a-wp-drafting-phase.md`](REFERENCE/01.0a-wp-drafting-phase.md).

## How it works

Each space (`WP` / `EC` / `D`) has a **`high-water`** — the highest number that
was already allocated when this ledger was adopted (2026-07-25). Numbers at or
below the high-water are grandfathered (they live in WORK_INDEX / EC_INDEX /
DECISIONS and are stable). **Every new allocation above the high-water gets one
reservation line here**, newest last:

```
- WP-422 — <kebab-slug or short title> (YYYY-MM-DD, <branch-or-PR>)
```

**The protocol (reserve first):**

1. `node scripts/check-number-ledger.mjs --next wp` (or `ec` / `d`) → the next free number.
2. Append its reservation line under the matching `## ` section **in your SPEC
   commit**, and get that tiny append merged **first** — claim the number before
   the bulky work.
3. `node scripts/check-number-ledger.mjs --check` must pass (CI runs it too).

**Why this holds under concurrency:**

- `.gitattributes` marks this file `merge=union`, so two sessions reserving
  *different* numbers auto-merge with **no conflict** on local rebase/merge (the
  big prose indices do not — that is why reservations live in this minimal file).
- If two sessions reserve the **same** number, union-merge keeps both identical
  lines and `--check` **fails loudly** (`DUPLICATE reservation`) — the collision
  surfaces early in CI, not silently at merge time. One session renumbers.
- `--check` also fails on **drift**: a number used in an index above the
  high-water with no reservation here (`UNRESERVED`).

Union-merge is a *local* git driver (it does not run on GitHub's server-side
squash) — that is fine: the duplicate check is the real safety net and catches a
same-number race however the merge happened.

- WP-465 — score-submit-rejection-copy (honest per-reason copy for the post-match score-submission banner: today useCompetitiveSubmitOnGameover blanket-maps every non-200 from POST /api/competition/scores to `'failed'` with the message "Couldn't submit your score. It may still be counted shortly." — misleading because the hasSubmitted guard means there is NO retry, and the dominant real cause is `par_not_published` (an ad-hoc non-ranked-gauntlet match is permanently ineligible, not a transient failure). SubmitScoreResult.error ALREADY carries the server rejection reason (readErrorCode), so the fix is client-only, zero-API: add a new SubmissionStatus `'ineligible'`, map result.error === 'par_not_published' → 'ineligible' (else 'failed'), add its honest copy + a neutral/info CSS variant in PlayViewport.vue, drop the false "may still be counted shortly" promise from the 'failed' copy, and harden SUBMISSION_MESSAGES to Record<Exclude<SubmissionStatus,'idle'>,string> so a new status forces its message; touches useCompetitiveSubmitOnGameover.ts + .test.ts + PlayViewport.vue; finer per-reason copy for the rare permanent reasons (not_owner/visibility_not_eligible/replay_not_found/match_not_finished) deferred, they keep 'failed'; NO server change, NO D-entry presentation-only like WP-459; hard-deps WP-339 (submit-after-match); 2026-07-30, SPEC-score-submit-copy)

- WP-470 — doombot-scry-interactive-choice (the deferred WP-447 phase-2 interactive follow-on Jeff requested from a live Magneto match: Doombot Legion's "Fight: Look at the top two cards of your deck. KO one of them and put the other back." currently AUTO-resolves (villainEffectScryKoOwnDeck KOs the deterministic worst via selectScryKoTarget), giving no player agency — line 172 KO'd a real "Dangerous Rescue" when both top cards were heroes. Upgrades scry-ko to an INTERACTIVE choice mirroring the WP-242/243 KO-a-Hero pending-choice infrastructure: villainEffectScryKoOwnDeck parks a new PendingScryKoChoice{choiceType:'scry-ko', playerID, revealedCardIds:[top≤2]} onto a G.pendingScryKoChoices FIFO queue when deck≥2 (1 → auto-KO, 0 → no-op, mirroring WP-242's 0/1/≥2); a resolveScryKoChoice bgio move (client:false, registered in game.ts) validates front playerID/choiceType + cardId∈revealedCardIds, KOs the chosen card leaving the other on top, front-pops; a hasPendingScryKoChoice block-all guard added to every action-move guard site (game.ts, coreMoves.impl, dodgeCard, fightMastermind, …) + the End-Turn/advanceStage gates; a UIState projection pendingScryKoChoice (+ the WP-249/D-24020 filtered private variant) showing the ≤2 revealed cards; the bot/sim path (ai.legalMoves + simulation.runner + par.aggregator) resolves DETERMINISTICALLY via selectScryKoTarget so par/replay are faithful; and the arena-client PendingScryKoChoicePrompt.vue (mirrors PendingKoHeroChoicePrompt.vue) + useTurnActions + uiMoveName wiring + PlayViewport mount. Cross-layer (game-engine + arena-client), engine-then-client execution order. Determinism: bot-driven fixtures fighting a Doombot now park→resolve (2 moves vs 1 auto-KO) + a new hashed G.pendingScryKoChoices field, so a fixture re-pin is LIKELY + game.test.ts move-set/count +1; reserves D-24282; hard-deps WP-447 (auto scry-ko) + WP-242/WP-243 (KO-a-Hero pending-choice pattern); 2026-07-30, SPEC-doombot-scry-interactive)

SCAFFOLD-FIRST IS MANDATORY: the blast radius is unknown BY CONSTRUCTION because these files have never been compiled, so the WP body is written only after an observed run, and an overflow too large for one packet is split rather than suppressed. Tooling-only: no src non-test file edited, pnpm -r build byte-identical, both sentinel hashes untouched. Post-WP-561 the baseline now CREDITS this work its full observed weight instead of zero. - WP-561 - in-play-hollow-baseline-rebuild (RESERVED: the /coverage in-play metric is STRUCTURALLY unable to reward the work it is meant to prioritise, and one-time baseline rebuild fixes it. D-24050 defines percentResolved = resolvedObs/totalObs where each mechanic contributes peakObs = max(committed baseline peakObs, live sweep hitCount), and resolvedObs counts a mechanic ONLY when its hero-ledger status is exactly `executable`. But an executable mechanic logs NO live hollows, so its peakObs collapses to its COMMITTED BASELINE value - and `apps/dashboard/src/data/in-play-hollow-baseline.json` is a hand seed [`generatedAt: "seed"`] of 14 mechanics / 140 total obs, recorded when the sweep was measuring games that died at ~turn 0. MEASURED 2026-08-16 on main: resolvedObs 59 comes ENTIRELY from three baseline entries [dodge 37 + undercover 20 + size-changing 2] while totalObs 2285 is now dominated by the post-WP-453 live sweep [2219 obs / 31 mechanics]. Numerator and denominator are therefore measured on DIFFERENT INSTRUMENTS. Consequence: implementing a top gap credits only whatever the stale seed happened to hold - `teleport` [live 178] credits 0, `outwit` [157] credits 0, `artifact` [169] credits 1, `investigate` [293] credits 5, `coordinate` [212] credits 7 - so the score ticks up only by DELETING observations from the denominator, i.e. the metric rewards a fix by erasing the evidence of it. That is precisely the failure D-24050's baseline mechanism exists to prevent; the mechanism is right, the DATA is stale by ~16x. SCOPE: rebuild the committed baseline once against the current honest sweep [after which the top-10 mechanics, 1689 of 2285 obs = 73.9%, become creditable: implementing them would move the metric 2.6% -> 76.5%]; re-pin the `useInPlayCoverage` snapshot test; amend D-24050 with a REBUILD TRIGGER [rebuild whenever sweep fidelity changes materially - backdrop, shuffle, turn depth - or numerator and denominator silently drift onto different instruments again, which is exactly what WP-453 caused]; and correct the two /coverage headline SUBTITLES, which today read as comparable percentages of the same thing when 36.1% is ROW-weighted [235 executable of 651 card x mechanic rows] and 2.6% is OBSERVATION-weighted [59 of 2285 in-play hollow observations]. HONEST NON-GOAL: the rebuild does NOT move today's 2.6% [denominator unchanged at 2285, numerator unchanged at 59] - it fixes the TRAJECTORY, not the optic. Any change that moves the number by re-baselining downward is explicitly forbidden. ewiki `wiki/dashboard.md` updates in LOCKSTEP, describing the MECHANISM not the numbers [numbers drift, mechanisms do not]. Dashboard + coverage feeds; NO engine, `G`, gameplay or determinism change. Reserves EC-596 + D-24370; amends D-24050 [WP-274]; hard-dep WP-453/D-24273 [the sweep that made the staleness visible]; 2026-08-16, claude/baseline-reserve)
- WP-565 - vp-icon-marker-mismap (RESERVED: 35 occurrences across 12 card sets render a card's VICTORY POINT value as a PIERCING icon, so the rules text a player reads names the wrong resource. Found while reviewing a real solo Red Skull / Super Hero Civil War match 2026-08-17: Supreme HYDRA displays "is worth +3[icon:piercing] for each other HYDRA Villain in your Victory Pile" where the printed card reads +3 VP. ROOT CAUSE is a single line - convert-cards-v15.mjs ICON_SLUG_MAP maps 4 to 'piercing' - and an audit of ALL 22 upstream `{ icon: 4 }` uses across the source files found EVERY ONE in a victory-point context ("is worth +N", "worth N", "printed [VP]", "equal to its printed [VP]"); not one is a combat-piercing use, so icon 4 is a second VP glyph and the mapping is simply wrong. SCORING IS UNAFFECTED and this is DISPLAY-ONLY: the engine never reads the marker (no `icon:piercing` consumer in packages/game-engine/src), and the same match proves the variable-VP maths is already correct - Supreme HYDRA scored 15 VP = 3 + 3 x 4 other HYDRA Villains, reconciling the 23 villainVP and the 52 totalVP exactly. Scope: fix the map, regenerate card data, and confirm no card LOSES a legitimate piercing reference. Note the 4 outlier sets (2099, amwp, wpnx, wtif) and hand-authored co2e do not flow through convert-cards-v15, so their occurrences need checking separately - 35 generated vs 22 upstream is that gap. A card-data change stales three derived feeds (mechanics:metadata, ledger:heroes, effect-index) which MUST regenerate in ONE commit or main reddens for every concurrent session. - WP-566 - blocked-ability-message-misattribution (RESERVED: ONE generic log line at hero/heroEffects.execute.ts:393 - "ability did not activate - a play condition (such as Hero class or team synergy) was not met" - is emitted for EVERY hero-hook condition failure, whatever the actual gate was. Reported from a real solo Red Skull / Super Hero Civil War match 2026-08-17 where it fired 8 times on Surge of Power, whose printed text is "If you made 8 or more recruit this turn, you get +3 attack" - a recruit threshold with NO class or team gate at all - while the SAME string was simultaneously CORRECT for Keen Senses, Odinson and Frenzied Slashing, which really are [hc:instinct]/[hc:strength] gates. Verified at source: evaluateCondition (hero/heroConditions.evaluate.ts) recognizes exactly FIVE condition types - heroClassMatch, requiresTeam, requiresKeyword, playedThisTurn, distinctHeroClassesAtLeast - and its `default` branch returns FALSE, so an UNRECOGNIZED condition type permanently blocks the ability and emits this same class/team wording; HeroCondition is a stringly-typed {type: string; value: string}, so nothing stops an unknown type being constructed. That makes the string a silent-failure amplifier: a data or parse bug is indistinguishable in the log from a legitimate synergy gate. Scope: name the gate that actually failed. OPEN QUESTION for drafting, deliberately NOT pre-decided: whether the `default: false` silent block should also become loud (it is arguably a separate defect and may deserve its own packet). NOTE what could NOT be verified this session: which condition type Surge of Power's recruit gate parses to - `recruit-threshold` appears in the card data marker but has NO engine implementation under that name, and a mock-registry parse returned no hooks. That unknown is itself the argument for the packet - with full repo access the log still did not say which gate failed. - WP-567 - red-skull-tactic-onfight-resolvers (RESERVED: Red Skull's FOUR Mastermind Tactics all have NO onFight resolver, so defeating any of them does literally nothing - and not even a log line, because dispatchTacticOnFight (rules/tacticHandlers.ts) falls unhandled tactic ids through to a SILENT no-op. WP-497 built the framework and WP-506 wired core Magneto's Crushing Shockwave into it; the engine still has exactly TWO resolvers total (that one plus co2e Doctor Octopus's Octet of Valence Electrons). Confirmed live in a solo Red Skull / Super Hero Civil War match at gitSha 47e5162 where the operator defeated ALL FOUR tactics and received nothing from any: the diagnostics show economy.recruit 5 at game end - exactly Officer +2 / Agent +1 / Surge of Power +2 - with no sign of Endless Resources' printed +4. The four ext_ids and their printed Fight effects: core-mastermind-red-skull-negablast-grenades [+3 attack, trivial]; core-mastermind-red-skull-endless-resources [+4 recruit, trivial]; core-mastermind-red-skull-hydra-conspiracy [draw two, then one more per HYDRA Villain in your Victory Pile - a COUNT-SCALED draw]; and core-mastermind-red-skull-ruthless-dictator [look at the top three of your deck, KO one, discard one, put one back on top - INTERACTIVE]. The Ruthless Dictator line is the risk and should be scoped deliberately: it parks a pending choice, and a parked choice shipped WITHOUT its UIState projection and prompt HARD-FREEZES the human player, so it either ships projection + prompt + bot enumeration mirror together or it defers to its own packet. DETERMINISM: the sentinel fixture is core/dr-doom, not Red Skull, so NO hash re-pin is expected on either oracle - verify rather than assume. Found by reviewing a real match against printed card text. Reserves EC-602 + D-24376; hard-dep WP-497; 2026-08-17, claude/reserve-tactics-waitsee)
- WP-568 - wait-and-see-conditional-semantics (RESERVED: OPERATOR DESIGN DECISION 2026-08-17 - a hero condition of the form "If you [did X] this turn" is a WAIT-AND-SEE window evaluated across the whole turn, not a snapshot taken at the moment the card is played. Thor's Surge of Power ["If you made 8 or more recruit this turn, you get +3 attack"] therefore grants its +3 attack RETROACTIVELY once the turn's recruit total reaches 8, even if the recruit arrives after Surge of Power was already in play - e.g. from defeating Red Skull's Endless Resources tactic [+4 recruit] later in the same turn. The operator's reading: "this turn" is a whole-turn window and attack is a turn-scoped resource, so a late grant is coherent. RECORDED FOR THE FUTURE READER - the drafting recommendation was the OPPOSITE [evaluate on play] on the grounds that Legendary resolves a card's ability when played, has no continuous re-check machinery, and that the rulebook's freedom to choose play order exists BECAUSE conditions check at resolution [making the build-recruit-then-play sequencing the card's skill component]; the operator OVERRULED that deliberately and this note exists so the decision reads as made rather than missed. SCOPE IS NOT ONE CARD: 31 ability lines across 17 sets share the "did you X this turn" shape [e.g. "If you played at least 8 other cards this turn", "If you drew any cards this turn"], a MIX of hero play-conditions and villain Fight effects, and the semantics must apply consistently rather than being special-cased for Surge of Power. Engineering shape to settle at drafting: a re-evaluation trigger on resource change vs a deferred-grant ledger; whether any new G state is needed [if so it is HASHED and BOTH oracles re-pin - budget for it]; and the interaction with WP-566/D-24375, since a condition that is "not yet met" is a DIFFERENT log state from one that failed and must not reuse the blocked wording. Reserves EC-603 + D-24377; sibling WP-566; 2026-08-17, claude/reserve-tactics-waitsee)
- WP-569 - mock-move-context-events-api (RESERVED: the SECOND packet of the WP-563 engine-test-typecheck arc - drive the `TS2345` class to ZERO. WP-563 shipped `tsconfig.test.json` + `typecheck:tests` and left 674 errors inventoried; `TS2345` is the largest single code at 180 across 17 files. SCAFFOLDED AT DRAFT TIME with `--noErrorTruncation` rather than predicted: 159 of the 180 are ONE root cause - hand-rolled mock move-context literals whose `events` object carries only `{ endTurn, setPhase, endGame }` while boardgame.io's `EventsAPI` also requires `endPhase, endStage, pass, setActivePlayers, setStage`. CRITICAL SIZING CORRECTION the raw error count hides: those 159 errors are CALL SITES of ~17 per-file local helpers (`createMockMoveContext` / `makeMoveContext`), so the fix is roughly SEVENTEEN literal edits, not 159 - the WP-563 backlog row's `180 / 17 files` reads as far larger than the change actually is. DESIGN FORK the WP must settle, with a recommendation and evidence: the five missing events are exactly the ones the architecture FORBIDS the engine from calling - a grep of all non-test engine source shows only `events.endTurn` (47) and `events.setPhase` (9), matching `.claude/rules/architecture.md` Phase & Turn Transitions - so stubbing them as THROWING rather than no-op turns a compiler fix into a live invariant gate, which is the D-24372 section 3 spirit (fix the test so it asserts something TRUE, never merely silence it). Remaining 21 errors are four small sub-classes taken in the same packet so the whole code reaches zero: 14 `SetupContext` not assignable to `UIBuildContext` (12 in uiState.build.progress.test.ts, 2 in uiState.filter.test.ts), 5 stale `{ listCards }` registry mocks missing `listSets`/`getSet` (the same stale-fixture shape WP-563 fixed in the drift-pin file), 1 `EffectNode | undefined`, and 1 where a test asserts `hook.keywords.includes('spectrum')` is FALSE while `HeroKeyword` makes that literal unrepresentable - the assertion is semantically right and the type system will not let it be asked, which is its own small finding. EXPLICIT NON-GOAL: consolidating the ~30 duplicate per-file mock helpers into `src/test/mockCtx.ts`. That is a real follow-up and is deliberately NOT bundled - it is a shared test abstraction touching 30 files, a different change in kind from completing a type surface, and it would put a 01.6 trigger inside a mechanical packet. CI wiring stays deferred (D-24372 section 2) until the count is zero. Reserves EC-604 + D-24378; hard-dep WP-563; 2026-08-17, claude/wp569-mockctx-draft)
- WP-570 - engine-test-possibly-undefined-indexing (RESERVED: the THIRD packet of the WP-563 engine-test-typecheck arc - drive the `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` class to ZERO. After WP-569 the gate stands at **498 errors**, of which **209 across 12 files** are `TS2532` "Object is possibly 'undefined'" (178) plus `TS18048` "'x' is possibly undefined" (31) - now the largest remaining class. **SCAFFOLDED AT DRAFT TIME, not predicted:** classifying every one of the 209 by its SOURCE LINE shows **154 are the single shape `playerZones[...]` index access**, and rewriting just that shape in the ONE biggest file (`hero/heroEffects.execute.test.ts`, 136 sites) with a mechanical regex moved the gate **498 -> 362** and the class **209 -> 73**. One file, one regex, 136 errors. The remaining 55 are array-index (`errors[0].field`, `faceDownCards[0].instanceId`) and already-bound-variable (`player0.deckCount`) forms of the same root cause. **THE KEY CORRECTNESS ARGUMENT, and the reason this is not a suppression:** the non-null assertion `!` has **NO runtime semantics** - TypeScript erases it and `tsx` strips it - so the rewrite is provably behavior-preserving; and the tests currently dereference these expressions unguarded (`gameState.playerZones['0'].hand.length`), so a genuinely-undefined value would ALREADY throw a TypeError today. The suite passing is itself the proof the values are defined. `!` is therefore NOT in the D-24372 section 3 banned set (`any` / `@ts-ignore` / `@ts-expect-error`), it is the repo's own established idiom - `playerZones['0']!` already appears across **44** engine test files - and this packet makes that consistent rather than introducing it. **TWO idioms to lock at drafting:** inline `!` for a one-off index access; and a single `assert.ok(value !== undefined, '<message>')` after binding when an indexed value is bound to a variable and read repeatedly, because node:test's `assert.ok` narrows and one assertion then covers every later use with a real failure message instead of a bare TypeError. **Out of scope:** the missing-required-state-field class (`TS2739`/`TS2741`); the ~90-error long tail; CI wiring (D-24372 section 2, still not zero); any non-test `src` file; and relocating the WP-569 loadout assertions to the builder seam. Reserves EC-605 + D-24379; hard-dep WP-563 + WP-569; 2026-08-17, claude/wp570-undefined-draft)
- WP-571 - engine-test-fixture-builders (RESERVED: the FOURTH packet of the WP-563 engine-test-typecheck arc, and the one WP-563 called **the finding, not the chore** - close the missing-required-state-field class (`TS2739` 113 + `TS2741` 82 = **195 errors / 49 files**), which exists because engine tests construct **structurally invalid `LegendaryGameState` values and pass**. SCAFFOLDED AT DRAFT TIME: the 195 collapse to **SIX types with a FIXED missing-field set each** - `PlayerZones` 47 (`faceDownCards`), `CardStatEntry` 32 (`fightCostMode` + `fightCostBase`), `MastermindState` 28 (`strikePile` + `attachedBystanders`), `TurnEconomy` 26 (`piercing` + `woundsDrawn`), `GlobalPiles` 24 (`horrors`), `CardRegistryReader` 19 (`listSets` + `getSet`) = 176, plus ~16 UI-projection types and 3 one-offs. **THE DECISIVE EVIDENCE, and why this is builders rather than 195 inline field additions:** these did NOT accumulate from one mistake. `git log -S` dates `faceDownCards` to **WP-282** and `horrors` to **WP-156** - separate packets, months apart - and there are at least SIX such events. Every one added a required field and left the fixtures invalid, undetected because nothing compiled them. Adding the fields inline restores the exact conditions for a seventh; a builder with defaults means the NEXT required field is added in ONE place. **Applies the operator's WP-569 precedent** (shared test-support module under `src/test/`, accepting that it ships in `dist`) rather than re-opening it - the situation is the same and 49 copies is far past the code-style threshold. **EXPLICITLY OUT OF SCOPE and verified separate at draft time:** the 4 deliberate narrow-registry `TS2345` sites in `buildInitialGameState.loadout.test.ts` that WP-569 recorded as a finding - they are `TS2345`, not this class, and `loadout.test.ts` does NOT appear among the 19 `CardRegistryReader` sites here. Also out: relaxing any production type to accept a partial fixture (the fixtures are wrong, not the types); CI wiring (D-24372 section 2 - after this the gate is ~94, still not zero); the ~90-error long tail. Reserves EC-606 + D-24380; hard-dep WP-563 + WP-569 + WP-570; 2026-08-17, claude/wp571-fixtures-draft)
- WP-572 - ast-aware-fixture-migration (RESERVED: finishes what WP-571 started, as a TOOLING packet rather than another sweep. WP-571 shipped six fixture builders and routed 176 literals, taking the gate 289 -> 134, but its AC-2 mutation broke **SIX** places instead of one and its AC-1 left 23 errors - because an ERROR-DRIVEN migration structurally cannot see literals that were ALREADY COMPLETE, and a REGEX cannot distinguish a value literal from a type annotation (a first codemod emitted syntax errors across 102 files; a second broke 18 tests on deep-equality assertions - both reverted). **PREMISE SCAFFOLDED AND VERIFIED AT DRAFT TIME, not inherited:** a TypeScript compiler-API probe using `checker.getContextualType` on every object literal in `tsconfig.test.json` resolves the six target types precisely, and crucially DISTINGUISHES `Record<string, PlayerZones>` (the parent map) from `PlayerZones` (the element) - exactly the precision regex lacked. Measured program-wide: **230 literals of the six types across 59 files**, of which **173 are already routed** through a builder (WP-571's work) and **57 are NOT** - and those 57 are precisely the already-complete literals that made WP-571's mutation break six places rather than one. `typescript` is already a devDependency, so no new dependency is added. Scope: route all 57, add builders for the residual UI-projection types (~19 errors, `UICityCard` and siblings) and the two simulation ctx literals, then RE-RUN WP-571's AC-2 mutation and require **exactly ONE** break (the production builder). Out of scope: the deliberately-narrow registry mocks that must STAY erroring (D-24378 and its second instance in `ui/uiState.build.test.ts` - completing them silences a guard the tests exist to prove fires); widening any production type; the ~90-error long tail; CI wiring. - WP-574 - master-strike-bystander-capture-log (RESERVED: every Master Strike silently captures a Bystander onto the Mastermind. `mastermindStrikeHandler` calls `captureBystanderOntoMastermind` for EVERY strike and EVERY mastermind, and that helper's SUCCESS path logs NOTHING - its only `pushLog` sits on the empty-supply branch. Reported from a real solo Red Skull / Super Hero Civil War match: the player rescued **3** Bystanders from the mastermind on turn 8 with only **ONE** capture logged (7.1.1, the villain-deck one); the other two arrived from the turn-3 and turn-6 Master Strikes with no log line at all, so three Bystanders appeared in the victory pile from nowhere. The asymmetry is the tell: the RESCUE is logged ("rescued 3 bystander(s) from the mastermind") while the CAPTURES are not. NOT a bug against D-15401: that decision (Immutable) specified a message only for the empty-supply case, so the silence is as-designed - this WP is ADDITIVE and contradicts nothing in it, which is why the fix is a log line rather than a decision amendment. Scope: one `pushLog` on the success path, `applied` per the WP-434 taxonomy, naming the capturing mastermind (the captured card is a generic `pile-bystander` supply token, so a count/attribution reads better than an id); the existing empty-supply line stays byte-unchanged. Determinism: messages are EXCLUDED from the sentinel oracle (`hashGameState`, D-24081) and `PRE_WP080_HASH` replays an empty move list with no strike, so BOTH oracles are expected byte-unchanged - the same two-hash analysis WP-566 verified empirically. Sibling of WP-566/D-24375 (log fidelity) but on a DIFFERENT path (mastermind strike, not hero hook), so no sequencing conflict. Reserves EC-609 + D-24383; 2026-08-17, claude/reserve-ms-bystander-log)
- WP-575 - diagnostics-report-carries-no-effect-trace (RESERVED: a Play Diagnostics report from a full match contains NO behavioural record of what any played card did. Observed twice - a 12-turn 1p match and a 24-turn 2p match - both exporting `entryCount: 0`, `entryDroppedCount: 0`, `truncated: false`. **THE BUFFER IS NOT BROKEN, and an executing session must not 'fix' it.** Verified at source: `installDiagnosticCapture()` runs at boot (`apps/arena-client/src/main.ts`), the capture wraps console.log/info/warn/error/debug plus window error + unhandledrejection, caps at DIAGNOSTIC_BUFFER_CAP 200 and discloses overflow via `truncated`. Its ~57 client call sites are overwhelmingly FAILURE paths (29 warn / 17 error / 11 log / 8 info / 4 debug), so a clean error-free match legitimately hits none and `entryCount: 0` is the CORRECT reading. **The real gap is that the report carries the wrong CHANNEL for its own stated purpose.** The buffer's docstring says it exists to make a "froze after I played X" report name its own cause - but the channel that actually records what each played card dispatched is `G.diagnostics.traces[]` (per-dispatch `EffectTrace`, WP-488 / D-24294, runtime-only, hash-excluded, capped at EFFECT_TRACES_CAP with a `tracesDropped` counter), and that channel NEVER reaches the report: it is not projected to `UIState`, and the client builds the report from `uiStateSnapshot`. So both reviewed matches - each containing exactly the "why did nothing happen" questions the report exists to answer - produced a report with zero console entries and zero effect traces, i.e. no behavioural record at all. Cross-layer by necessity (engine projection + client report), the WP-410 precedent. OPEN QUESTION for drafting, deliberately NOT pre-decided: HOW to surface traces without shipping a heavy cross-audience channel on every frame - a diagnostics-only projection, an owner-redacted `UIState` field, or a server-side export are all live options, and the choice changes the packet's layer surface. Determinism: traces are already hash-excluded and a projection adds no `G` field, so both oracles are expected byte-unchanged - but the `UIState` drift pin MUST be extended (an optional add passes the existing keyset assertion silently, the WP-562 lesson) and any new field MUST pass `filterUIStateForAudience`, which rebuilds field-by-field and silently drops what it does not know. Reserves EC-610 + D-24384; deferred from WP-562 section Notes; 2026-08-18, claude/reserve-diagnostics-entries)
- WP-576 - civil-war-2p-hero-count-requirement (RESERVED: closes the deferred REQUIREMENT half of Super Hero Civil War's printed "If only 2 players, use only 4 Heroes in the Hero Deck." WP-515/D-24328 shipped only the ENGINE build DOWNSIZE (`resolveEffectiveHeroDeckIds` slices the built deck 5->4 at 2p) and DELIBERATELY left the requirement/validation/builder side at 5, flagging a "displays 5, plays 4" caveat as out-of-scope. That gap is now a LIVE UX defect the operator hit: the cards builder (cards.legendary-arena.com) requires 5 hero decks at 2p Civil War and GRAYS OUT the LAGN / MATCH-SETUP download when only 4 are selected, contradicting the printed card. Fix (the OPPOSITE class from a downsize; mirrors WP-524/D-24337's Secret Invasion "6 Heroes" requirement override EXACTLY): add a `core/super-hero-civil-war` + `numPlayers===2` -> 4 branch to registry `resolveEffectiveHeroCount`, the single source every hero-count checker reaches - `checkPlayerCountComposition` (registry) + `validatePlayerCountComposition` (game-engine `matchSetup.validate`, the `Game.setup` gate) + the registry-viewer builder instruction / download-gate / preview - so all agree on 4 in lockstep. Then RECONCILE the engine downsize: with the requirement now guaranteeing exactly 4 ids at 2p, `resolveEffectiveHeroDeckIds`' `slice(0,4)` becomes a defensive no-op; KEEP it as a guard but correct its stale "matchSetup.validate requires exactly 5 at 2p" comment. Locked behaviour: require EXACTLY 4 at 2p (a 5-hero 2p Civil War loadout now FAILS validation); 3-5p Civil War and every other scheme unchanged (flat table). VALIDATION-CHANGE class -> SCAFFOLD-FIRST: prototype the branch and run the registry / game-engine / registry-viewer suites to surface every 5-hero-2p-Civil-War fixture that now fails (`buildInitialGameState.civilWarSizing.test.ts` is the prime candidate) and fold the fixture migration into scope. Determinism: alters the hero-deck build for this scheme+2p only via a 4-id loadout; no committed fixture uses Civil War 2p (sole complete-game fixture is core/dr-doom / Legacy Virus) -> finalStateHash / PRE_WP080 byte-identical expected (verify, re-pin only on a real fixture diff). Cross-layer (registry authority + engine reconcile + viewer tests) -> standard two-session lane. Reserves EC-611 + D-24385; hard-dep WP-515/D-24328 [engine downsize, merged] + WP-524/D-24337 [`resolveEffectiveHeroCount` requirement-side precedent, merged] + WP-370/D-24165 [PLAYER_COUNT_SETUP]; 2026-08-17, claude/civilwar-2p-heroreq-draft)
- WP-577 - red-skull-strike-interactive-ko-choice (RESERVED: Red Skull's Master Strike prints "Each player KOs a Hero from their hand" - an owning-player CHOICE - but WP-386/D-24188 shipped a deterministic AUTO-PICK (each player's lowest-cost hand Hero) as an explicit MVP "to avoid a blocking multi-player pending-choice," and D-24188 itself names the follow-up "a future WP may upgrade to a per-player KO-target prompt." Operator hit it live reviewing a 2p Red Skull / Super Hero Civil War match. Fix: the D-24284 SPLIT the engine already uses for every "each player" effect - the ACTIVE player (whose turn the strike fired on) gets an interactive prompt to pick their KO'd Hero; NON-ACTIVE allies auto-pick lowest-cost hand Hero (unchanged). REUSES the shipped `ko-hero` interactive cluster end-to-end (`PendingKoHeroChoice` + `resolveKoHeroChoice` + `UIPendingKoHeroChoice` + `PendingKoHeroChoicePrompt.vue` + the `ai.legalMoves` bot fallback), hand-SCOPED (Red Skull is hand-only) via an additive optional marker on `PendingKoHeroChoice` (absent = unchanged all-zone villain `ko-hero`). NO new move (move-count unchanged), no new pending kind / UIState field / prompt component. Bot current-player auto-resolves BYTE-IDENTICAL to the old `selectRedSkullKoTarget` (lowest-cost hand, tie -> lowest index) so sims + both hash oracles are unchanged; no committed fixture reaches Red Skull [sentinel is core/dr-doom]. OUT OF SCOPE: full non-active-player interactivity [the first such in the engine - needs the play phase `activePlayers` restructured so a non-active seat can submit a move + a multi-entry projection; a later WP]. Supersedes the interactive half of D-24188 (its auto-pick stays the ally + bot fallback). Cross-layer [engine resolver + reused cluster + client prompt] -> standard lane. Reserves EC-612 + D-24386; supersedes D-24188; reuses WP-532/D-24284+D-24343 [the current-parks/allies-auto split] + the shipped ko-hero cluster; 2026-08-19, claude/redskull-strike-choice)
- WP-578 - surface-competitive-score-on-endgame (RESERVED: the competitive final score (rawScore - parScore, lower-is-better, computed SERVER-SIDE by `parScoring.logic.ts` on replay ingest and stored in `legendary.competitive_scores`) is NEVER shown to the player. `buildParBreakdown` (`packages/game-engine/src/ui/uiState.build.ts`) hard-returns `undefined` under D-6701 ("payload deferred until buildUIState has a ReplayResult"), so `UIGameOverState.par` is always omitted and `EndgameSummary.vue` renders only outcome + in-game VP total ("Final scores recorded (N players)") - NOT the competitive score. To see their number today a player must `GET /api/me/scores` or open their profile. KEY FORK (D-24387): the score is server-derived from the replay, so the engine has no `ReplayResult` at gameover and CANNOT populate `par` - D-6701's premise stands; the fix is ARENA-CLIENT-side - after `useCompetitiveSubmitOnGameover` POSTs `{matchId}` and the server returns the breakdown (`buildScoreBreakdown`), render that final/raw/par score in `EndgameSummary`, gated on submit completion, with graceful fallback for guests (no submit) and unranked/casual matches. Confirm at framing whether the `POST /api/competition/scores` response carries the breakdown or a follow-up `GET /api/me/scores?matchId` is needed. Layer: arena-client only; no engine/persistence change, no hash surface. Found in a real Red Skull / Midtown Bank Robbery 2p match review - the winner asked "where is my score". Reserves EC-613 + D-24387; 2026-08-21, claude/draft-score-legends-recruit)
- WP-579 - leaderboard-handle-profile-link (RESERVED: the legends.legendary-arena.com leaderboard renders each row's player as PLAIN TEXT of `legendary.players.display_name` (`apps/legends-board` OverallPanel.vue / WeeklyPanel.vue / etc.), which is (a) not a link and (b) the account `display_name` - which FALLS BACK TO THE EMAIL LOCAL-PART - not the claimed URL-safe handle. A public profile page already exists at `play.legendary-arena.com/?profile=<handle>` backed by `GET /api/players/:handle/profile`, keyed on the claimed handle (`display_handle` / `handle_canonical`, migration 008, nullable until first claim). `competitive_scores` stores only `player_id`; identity is JOINed at publish time and `leaderboard.logic.ts` selects only `p.display_name`. This WP shows the claimed handle as a LINK to the profile. Touches: `leaderboard.logic.ts` query (+ `display_handle`/`handle_canonical`) and `PublicLeaderboardEntry`; the legends snapshot schema + builders (`legends.types.ts`, `legends.logic.ts`, which today set `handle: playerDisplayName`); and the legends-board panels (render `<a href=".../?profile=<handle>">`). KEY DECISION (D-24388): fallback for a ranked player who never claimed a handle - they have only `display_name` (not a valid profile URL key); options are plain-text display_name (no link) vs a claim nudge; and whether to STOP the email-local-part fallback reaching the public board (privacy). Layer: server (leaderboards read + legends publisher) + legends-board app; NOT lightweight-lane-eligible (leaderboard/identity surface); two-session; consider a server/app split if >10 files. - WP-580 - use-recruit-as-attack-conversion (RESERVED: God of Thunder (`core/thor/god-of-thunder`, `data/cards/core.json`) reads "You can use [icon:recruit] as [icon:attack] this turn" but the conversion is COMPLETELY UNIMPLEMENTED and silently so. The ability line carries NO `[keyword:]` marker (only decorative `[icon:]` tokens), so `parseAbilityText` (`setup/heroAbility.setup.ts`) resolves it to two no-op grant keywords (attack, recruit) with UNDEFINED magnitude, and `detectHollowHeroHook` does NOT flag it (attack/recruit are MVP_KEYWORDS with handlers -> hasReachable=true), so it masquerades as applied and never shows in diagnostics. SCOPE = core God of Thunder ONLY, recruit->attack one-directional, whole-turn; variants DEFERRED. DETERMINISM: a new `TurnEconomy` field is hash-bearing -> a `finalStateHash` + `PRE_WP080_HASH` dual re-pin, OR kept turn-reset/lazy to move neither oracle; engine skill applies; NOT lightweight-lane. Removes the invented bystander-rescue reward [ScoringWeights.bystanderReward] and rescales penalty weights to true rulebook VP-units [villainEscaped 100→10, schemeTwistNegative 300→30, bystanderLost 400→40; mastermindTacticUntaken/scenarioSpecificPenalty→10], so RawScore = penalties − VP×10 mirrors the rulebook VP − 4·lost − 3·twists − 1·escapes [rescued bystanders score once, as their 1 VP]. Removes ScoreBreakdown.weightedBystanderReward [client mirror→optional]. Updates 12-SCORING-REFERENCE + wiki/scoring + VISION §21. Reserves EC-634 + D-24409. 2026-08-24, worktree-rulebook-faithful-scoring)
- WP-600 — endgame-report-card-rulebook-display-polish (RESERVED: the WP-599 display follow-up, on the rulebook-faithful model. (1) rescued/lost TERMINOLOGY — never bare "bystanders"; the per-player split + PAR-derivation givens say "bystanders rescued". (2) a PENALTIES/AWARDS scoring-key legend near the grade scale showing each event's points [Awards: Victory point −10, incl. rescued bystanders at their 1 VP; Penalties: villain escaped +10, scheme twist +30, bystander lost +40]. (3) RAW SCORE + BY PLAYER restyle. Client-only [EndgameSummary.vue + scoreCalcDisplay.ts + gradeDisplay.ts]; no engine/scoring/server change. Reserves EC-635 + D-24410. Stacked on WP-599. 2026-08-24, worktree-rulebook-scoring-display)
- WP-601 — endgame-par-derivation-bystander-lost-term (RESERVED: display bug caught in D-24026 live-verify [real 2p Midtown/Red Skull match]. The endgame PAR-derivation formula omitted the expected-bystanders-lost penalty term, so the shown arithmetic did not reconcile to the printed PAR — it read "(1×10)+(6×30)−(74×10) = −470" where the expansion is −550, missing "+ (2×40)". WP-591/D-24400 put bystandersLostPar in computeParScore but never in buildParDerivation. Adds the bystanders-lost term to the derivation formula + substituted + an "Expected bystanders lost" given, mirroring the twist term. Client-only [scoreCalcDisplay.ts + EndgameSummary.vue]; no engine/scoring change; PAR value itself unchanged. Reserves EC-636 + D-24411. 2026-08-24, worktree-par-derivation-fix)
- WP-602 — bystander-reveal-notable-event (7th NotableGameEvent variant 'bystanderRevealed' emitted on the villain-deck bystander-capture reveal path so the center-screen NotableEventOverlay fires for a revealed Bystander like it does for Scheme Twist / Master Strike; engine notableEvents.types+compose+reveal.ts + finalStateHash re-pin + arena-client overlay chip/border/eventCardId. Cross-layer engine+client. RENUMBERED from WP-601 after PR #1625 [endgame-par-derivation] merged the same 601/636/24411 to main first. Reserves EC-637 + D-24412. 2026-08-24, claude/bystander-reveal-notable-event-body)
- WP-603 — melter-fight-interactive-ko-keep (upgrade core Melter [core/masters-of-evil/melter] Fight from the WP-519/D-24332 deterministic auto-resolve to the FAITHFUL interactive per-card KO/keep choice Jeff requested from a live 2p Midtown/Red Skull match: reveal each player's deck top, the fighting player is prompted to KO-or-keep EACH revealed card [their own + every ally's], a kept card returns face-up to the top of that player's deck. Reverses D-24332 [auto-resolve] to a pending-choice on the WP-470/D-24282 interactive scry-ko + WP-242 ko-hero model. Cross-layer engine [new pending-choice state + resolve move + bot getLegalMoves mirror + UIState build/filter pass-through] + arena-client [prompt component]. Reserves EC-638 + D-24413. 2026-08-24, claude/wp603-melter-interactive)

SUPERSEDES D-24278's per-mastermind axis, keeps 1 variant/leg + D-24199 core rule; PAR ~2,118 UNCHANGED [schemes already keyed distinct ScenarioKeys]; competitive_scores empty so zero migration. - D-24282 — doombot-scry-interactive-choice (scry-ko-own-deck (Doombot Legion Fight, WP-447 D-24267) upgrades from AUTO-resolve to an INTERACTIVE player choice for HUMAN players, mirroring the WP-242/243 KO-a-Hero pending-choice pattern: the villainEffectScryKoOwnDeck parker pushes a PendingScryKoChoice{choiceType:'scry-ko', playerID, revealedCardIds:[the top min(2,deck.length)]} onto a new runtime G.pendingScryKoChoices FIFO queue when deck≥2 and KOs nothing yet; a 1-card deck still auto-KOs (no choice) and 0 is a no-op (the WP-242 0/1/≥2 branch). resolveScryKoChoice({G,playerID},{cardId}) validates the FRONT entry (playerID + choiceType + cardId∈revealedCardIds), KOs the chosen card from the deck leaving the other on top, front-pops; all invalid states silent no-ops (moves never throw), queue byte-identical on no-op so the player resubmits. A hasPendingScryKoChoice predicate blocks every action move + the End-Turn/advanceStage gate while pending (the WP-242 block-all pattern) so the choice cannot be skipped — shipping the pending state WITHOUT this + the UIState projection + the client prompt would hard-freeze the client (project_pending_choice_no_ux_freeze), so all three ship together. UIState gains pendingScryKoChoice (front-of-queue, ≤2 revealed cards + a WP-249/D-24020 filtered private variant visible only to the choosing player). DETERMINISM PRESERVED for bots/sims: ai.legalMoves + simulation.runner + par.aggregator resolve the pending via selectScryKoTarget (the same worst-first card the old auto-resolve KO'd), so par/replay stay faithful and only live HUMAN play gets the prompt — but bot fixtures now park→resolve (2 moves vs 1) with a new hashed G.pendingScryKoChoices field, so a fixture re-pin is LIKELY and game.test.ts move-set +1 (resolveScryKoChoice). Cross-layer (game-engine parker/move/projection/guards/bot + arena-client PendingScryKoChoicePrompt.vue), single WP with per-layer allowlist + engine-then-client order; contract change (new pending-choice type + move + G field + UIState field) recorded by this D-entry; reserved by WP-470, DRAFTED here, landed at WP-470 execution; hard-deps WP-447 + WP-242/WP-243; 2026-07-30, SPEC-doombot-scry-interactive)
- D-24281 — villain-reveal-or-wound-primitive (the eighth villain effect primitive `reveal-or-wound` — a CONDITIONAL each-player effect parameterized by a hero-trait predicate {requireKind:'team'|'hero-class', requireValue}, appended to the closed VILLAIN_EFFECT_PRIMITIVES union + array at position 8 append-only with the drift test moved to 8; grammar reveal-or-wound:<team|hc>:<value> where hc/team mirror the card-text [hc:X]/[team:X] namespaces + the D-24076 [require-to-defeat:<kind>:<value>] grammar, hc→'hero-class' at parse; the descriptor gains requireKind + requireValue fields, requireValue normalized to the cardTraits slug space (normalizeTraitSlug). Semantics auto-resolved: for EACH player in Object.keys(playerZones).sort() the handler scans that player's HAND for a hero whose G.cardTraits {team,heroClass} matches and, only on NO match, applies gainWound (mirroring gain-wound:each's woundsDrawn bump when the wounded player is the current player) — a hand-only predicate DELIBERATELY narrower than D-24076 playerMeetsDefeatRequirement (hand+inPlay) because the printed 'reveal' is a hand action; a matching player reveals for free. No player choice (revealing to avoid the Wound is optimal for a Wound-averse player; the rare Wound-synergy decline is a documented deferral). Keyword-less descriptor so descriptorToLegacyKeyword→undefined and descriptorKey NOT extended, self-narrates via pushLog naming which players were wounded (scry-ko D-24267 precedent); frozen 10-keyword surface untouched. Marks CORE only 5 cards / 8 markers (sabretooth/frost-giant/zzzax each fight+escape, ymir ambush, ultron escape; every core Same-effect Escape marked) + removes stale _unassigned rows; ~35 cross-set instances data-only follow-on, conditional/compound variants deferred. - D-24306 — match-endgame-controls (Active (landed 2026-08-05): locks the End-Game / Play-Again model — [1] "End Game" is the MATCH_ENDED_EARLY endgame COUNTER, NOT boardgame.io events.endGame: the client-visible UIState.gameOver is derived by buildUIState from evaluateEndgame(G.counters), not ctx.gameover, so an events.endGame end would set ctx.gameover but NEVER surface the endgame panel [hard-freeze]. endMatchEarly [server-only move, client:false, NOT a CORE_MOVE_NAME; never throws; no-op when already over] sets the counter; evaluateEndgame checks it FIRST → { outcome:'tie', reason:'The players ended the match early.', endedEarly:true }; the top-level endIf ends the match for every seat. [2] endedEarly is an ADDITIVE optional field on EndgameResult + UIGameOverState [outcome STAYS 'tie'] — deliberately off the EndgameOutcome union to avoid its CompetitiveOutcome / migration-026 / dashboard / coop / campaign / sim blast radius. [3] An early-ended match is NEVER scored: server submitCompetitiveScoreImpl rejects endedEarly with a new ended_early SubmissionRejectionReason [union + SUBMISSION_REJECTION_REASONS array lockstep] BEFORE any INSERT [authoritative]; the client submit-on-gameover hook also skips the POST → 'ineligible' [advisory]. [4] "Play Again" relaunches SOLO with the same stashed loadout [readMatchSetup] + the same seat count [snapshot.players.length] via launchMatchFromComposition. No new top-level G field → no finalStateHash/PRE_WP080 re-pin [runtime-only counter]. Operator decisions: never-score-early-end + relaunch-solo. Scoped. - D-24354 — surge-of-power-recruit-threshold-conditional-attack (RESERVED: locks the fix for Thor Surge of Power's unconditional +3 attack [bug surfaced at WP-542's live-verify, match uONsXu1WnDR]. The card's +3[icon:attack] is a game-state-CONDITIONAL grant [only if the player made 8+ recruit this turn], but the inline-icon parse [heroAbility.setup.ts ICON_MAGNITUDE_PATTERN] produces an UNCONDITIONAL effect because no recruit-threshold HeroCondition exists. Locks: (1) a NEW HeroCondition type recruitMadeThisTurnAtLeast evaluated as G.turnEconomy.recruit >= parseInt(value) [recruit is the gross recruit-MADE accumulator, not net available — available = recruit - spentRecruit]; HeroCondition is open-typed {type,value} so this is a switch-case add, no union/drift/canonical-array change; (2) a marker to attach a condition to an inline-icon-magnitude effect line so the existing +3[icon:attack] grant becomes gated by evaluateAllConditions [the exact marker token is an execution-time detail; FORK for operator/copilot review — a bespoke [cond:...] token vs reusing/extending an existing keyword]; (3) scope is Surge of Power ONLY — the general "game-state conditional" deferred class stays deferred except for this one condition type. Determinism: reads G.turnEconomy [no ctx.random], adds no persistent shape; no committed fixture plays Surge of Power so finalStateHash/PRE_WP080 byte-identical [verify at execution]. Reserved by WP-545, DRAFTED at draft, landed at WP-545 execution; hard-dep WP-021 [HeroCondition] + WP-179 [condition evaluation]; 2026-08-14, claude/surge-conditional-attack)
- D-24355 — core-supreme-hydra-dynamic-victory-points (RESERVED: implements the FIRST card-text dynamic VP modifier — Supreme HYDRA [core/hydra/supreme-hydra] "worth +3 VP for each other HYDRA Villain in your Victory Pile" — as a scoring-time resolver [scoring/dynamicVictoryPoints.ts] folded into computeFinalScores' villainVP, NOT as a timed [effect:X] villain ability [scoring modifiers live in the scoring subsystem, keyed by card identity, never in the villain-ability parser]. [icon:piercing] = victory points in this data. Base 3 + 3 x other-hydra-villain-count; HYDRA-group membership derived from the core-villain-hydra- ext_id prefix; no new G snapshot; no PlayerScoreBreakdown field [folded into villainVP]; no card-data/marker/ledger change [stays "(unmarked)" in the timed-effect ledger like Blob's require-to-defeat]. - D-24357 — coverage-subsystem-covered-status (RESERVED: adds a NEW coverage status "subsystem" to the card-mechanic ledgers + effect-implementation-index + dashboard viewer, for cards implemented by a subsystem OTHER than the [effect:X] villain-ability pipeline — currently defeat-requirement cards [Blob/Venom/Zombie-Venom via the WP-292 require-to-defeat setup subsystem] and card-text VP modifiers [Supreme HYDRA via the WP-546 scoring subsystem]. Introduces a curated allowlist scripts/coverage/subsystem-coverage.json [card ext_id -> {subsystem,wp,decision}] that the villain-mechanic-ledger reads to emit "subsystem" [distinct from unmarked=todo and deferred=recognized-but-unimplemented; means done-elsewhere]. Threads the new status through the index generator STATUS_ORDER, the registry effectImplementationIndex Zod enum, and the dashboard LedgerStatus union + statusLabel + STATUS_ORDER + CSS [render as covered]. Tooling/observability only — NO engine, G, gameplay, or determinism change. Landed at WP-548 execution; 2026-08-14, WP-548)
- D-24358 — registry-viewer-lagn-result-roundtrip (RESERVED: locks that a Registry Viewer Loadout-tab LAGN export MUST NOT fabricate a match verdict. LAGN result is OPTIONAL [Tier-1 setup documents legitimately omit it], so an outcome that was never imported nor explicitly chosen is UNSET and the result block is OMITTED — a guessed "victory" asserts an authority the loadout builder does not have. An imported result.outcome [+ loss_condition] round-trips intact instead of being discarded at import and re-fabricated at export. loss_condition is never synthesized [the old code stamped "deck_exhausted" on every defeat, wrong for scheme-completion and mastermind losses]. The server producer apps/server matchLagn.logic.ts toLagnResult [heroes-win->victory / scheme-wins->defeat / tie->omit the block] remains the sole authority for a REAL match verdict and is unchanged. - D-24359 — maestro-zero-count-narration-split (RESERVED: supersedes the narration clause of D-24353 ONLY. The counted-self-KO handler must distinguish its two blocked outcomes: owed===0 [no trait-matching Hero, so the ability asked for nothing] narrates by naming the trait, following the ko-heroes-current-by-trait "KO-d 0 of your <trait> Hero(es)" precedent, while owed>0-with-no-eligible-target keeps the bare "no Heroes to KO". Both remain outcome "blocked" reachable no-ops, never hollow records. Rationale: a single shared message made a live log [match NPyIIWIjd1Q, 2026-08-15] read as "you had nothing to lose" when the truth was "you had no Strength Heroes". G.messages is hash-excluded [D-24081]: no determinism, persistence, or hash surface. Everything else D-24353 locked — the primitive name, the trait-supplies-the-COUNT semantic, the duplicated ko-hero park loop, the applied/neutral messages — is UNCHANGED. Landed at WP-550 execution; 2026-08-15, WP-550)
 [REVISED 2026-08-15 at drafting: pre-flight proved the "owed>0 with no eligible KO target" state is UNREACHABLE — countPlayerHeroesMatchingTrait counts only cards carrying a G.cardTraits entry and buildKoEligibleTargets excludes only WOUND_EXT_ID, so every counted Hero is itself KO-eligible and owedFromTrait===0 is the SOLE reachable blocked trigger. The design is therefore a REPLACE of the one message, NOT a two-branch split; no second branch is added. Also correcting the hash claim in this reservation: messages is excluded from finalStateHash (hashGameState) per D-24081 but STAYS hashed by computeStateHash (replay.hash.ts, pinned by a live test) — no re-pin arises because the PRE_WP080 replay runs moves:[] and no committed fixture fights Maestro.]
- D-24360 — loadout-import-format-sniff (RESERVED: locks that the Loadout tab three adjacent JSON importers must tell an operator WHICH box a file belongs in rather than dumping the wrong validator field-level errors. The sniff reads three mutually-exclusive top-level discriminators and is ADVISORY ONLY: it never auto-routes and never auto-loads, because silently loading a document into a box the operator did not choose is a worse surprise than an error message. An unrecognized or malformed shape falls through to the existing validator output unchanged, so no existing rejection path is weakened. Landed at WP-551 execution; 2026-08-15, WP-551)
- D-24361 — registry-viewer-deploy-version-check (RESERVED: locks that apps/registry-viewer gains its own build-stamped version.json + deploy-freshness check + reload banner, PORTING the WP-418/D-24238 arena-client pattern rather than extracting a shared package -- registry-viewer is the SECOND consumer, and duplicate-first/abstract-on-third governs [extract at a third]. The fetch is FAIL-SOFT by contract: any error, missing file, or unparseable body resolves to no-update, so a network blip can never produce a false "update available". Rationale: on 2026-08-15 a shipped-and-deployed WP-549 change was invisible to the operator because their browser held a cached bundle, and the viewer had no way to say so -- two exchanges were spent proving the deploy was live before the staleness was traced to the browser. Landed at WP-552 execution; 2026-08-15, WP-552)

As the SECOND dynamic-VP card, it earns the resolver's multi-card shape [duplicate-first satisfied]. Ultron's Escape [reveal-or-wound:hc:tech] is already implemented [WP-469] and out of scope; only the scoring modifier is added. Landed at WP-553 execution; 2026-08-15, WP-553)
- D-24363 — simulation-nontermination-turn-depth (RESERVED: records the root cause and fix for a simulated game failing to terminate past ~13 turns, plus the re-baselined runtime-observed sweep sizing. Establishes that the pre-WP-453 reverse-mock shuffle was MASKING this by killing games at turn 0 — the sweep budget documented as "~1 s / ~2.7 ms per game" was an artifact of that bug, not a real measurement — and that the correct response is a root-cause fix with re-measured sizing, never a MAX_TURNS cap chosen to make a CI gate green. Landed at WP-554 execution; 2026-08-15, WP-554)

- D-24364 - legalmoves-discard-to-play-payability (RESERVED: records the THIRD application of the D-24363 part-1 rule - enumeration mirrors the move guard - this time for the `discard-to-play` pre-commit precondition, and the empirical finding that WP-554's within-turn bound converted this from an invisible hang into a fast, localizable failure.
